(`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); غشيان الليل (`quranic:root_000266:B002/m01`); البستان المستور بالشجر (`quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`); التفاف النبات واندفاعه (`quranic:root_000266:B011/m01`); المَجَنَّة موضع الاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B017/m01`); أرض ونبت ريان (`quranic:root_000783:B014/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); غلة الشجر والزرع (`quranic:root_000043:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ك ل`: 69:24 (كُلُ), 69:37 (يَأْكُلُ)","synthesis":"Cover, night-like enclosure, tree garden, dense growth, and a place of shelter make the orchard an inhabited canopy. Moist wooded ground, ripening fruit, and crop yield supply its productive interior, so concealment is life-supporting rather than evasive.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:24","69:34","69:36","69:37"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: The garden lowers its harvest to the eater","parent_semantic_invariant":"Cultivation joins sheltering growth, vertical abundance, ripened fruit, timely cutting, and bodily access.","parent_surprising_reach":"Elevation is reversed into service: the high place does not distance its produce but bends it close, while past action appears as seed, advance, and first-flowing yield.","key":"C","name":"Height that bends into nearness","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"An elevated setting places its usable upper growth within reach of the eater.","active_motifs":"السمو والارتفاع (`quranic:root_001042:B001/m01`); الرفعة والشرف (`quranic:root_001042:B002/m01`); الجهة العليا ومن فوق (`quranic:root_001042:B005/m01`); الشيء المحمول على الأعلى (`quranic:root_001042:B008/m01`); الطول والضخامة (`quranic:root_001042:B010/m01`); القرب والمقاربة (`quranic:root_000493:B001/m01`); الدنيا والأدنى (`quranic:root_000493:B002/m01`); دنو الأعلى من الوسط (`quranic:root_000493:B005/m01`); الثمر المقطوف والعنقود الداني (`quranic:root_001241:B002/m01`); مشي الدابة المتقارب البطيء (`quranic:root_001241:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ)","synthesis":"Height, honor, upper side, top-load, and tall bodily form establish vertical abundance. Nearness, the lower world as what is close, and a bent upper body reverse distance; the slow close-stepping animal gives the same geometry motion, and the low cluster becomes the point where height meets hand.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:23"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Handedness organizes enclosure and omen","parent_semantic_invariant":"Right and left are not mere coordinates: they distribute blessing, force, enclosure, social cohesion, cold, and ominous rank.","parent_surprising_reach":"A side becomes a garment, wind, covered weapon, herd-binding device, and social 'gatheredness,' giving handed judgment a full material frame.","key":"C","name":"Wrapping, encompassing, and concealed load","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A side-cloth closes around body or object and may hide what is being carried.","active_motifs":"إحاطة واشتمال من الجوانب (`quranic:root_000819:B001/m01`); لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); حمل مستور في الاشتمال (`quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`); الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ)","synthesis":"Encompassing, cloak, tied udder or fruit cover, hidden short sword or stolen animal, generic concealment, shield, undergarment, and plush covering make sidedness into an enclosing textile operation. What the hand receives may be displayed, but what the garment holds can remain unseen.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:22","69:30","69:23"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Handedness organizes enclosure and omen","parent_semantic_invariant":"Right and left are not mere coordinates: they distribute blessing, force, enclosure, social cohesion, cold, and ominous rank.","parent_surprising_reach":"A side becomes a garment, wind, covered weapon, herd-binding device, and social 'gatheredness,' giving handed judgment a full material frame.","key":"D","name":"Reunited group and inherited disposition","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Scattered persons are gathered into one social whole with recognizable habits and rank.","active_motifs":"ائتلاف الشمل بعد تفرق (`quranic:root_000819:B002/m01`); شمائل الإنسان وخلائقه (`quranic:root_000819:B008/m01`); الشمال موضع شؤم أو خساسة (`quranic:root_000819:B013/m01`); سواد الناس وجماعتهم (`quranic:root_000266:B013/m01`); المراضاة والتراضي رضا متبادل (`quranic:root_000569:B003/m01`); الرضي صفة للمطيع أو المحب أو الضامن (`quranic:root_000569:B006/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ر ض و`: 69:21 (رَّاضِيَةٍ)","synthesis":"Reunited company, personal dispositions, unfavorable status, mass of people, mutual approval, and the obedient or guaranteeing associate describe the social 'side' to which one belongs. Cohesion is ethical as well as numerical.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:22","69:21"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Faith is tested as trust and provision","parent_semantic_invariant":"Belief is not isolated assent; it creates security, worshipful orientation, and active responsibility for another person's food and dwelling.","parent_surprising_reach":"Faith's lexical image is a calm heart holding a trust, while neglect of the poor is rendered as refusal to set desire, household, land, and food in motion toward them.","key":"G","name":"Hidden beings and the unseen religious field","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Religious assent situates human conduct within a field that includes concealed nonhuman beings.","active_motifs":"الجن المستترون (`quranic:root_000266:B005/m01`); التعبد والمعبود (`quranic:root_000047:B001/m01`); تصديق يطمئن إليه القلب (`quranic:root_000054:B002/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ء ل ه`: 69:33 (ٱللَّهِ); `ء م ن`: 69:33 (يُؤْمِنُ)","synthesis":"Hidden spirits, worship, and believing assent form a compact unseen religious scene. The latent spirit image sharpens the difference between principled faith in a trustworthy report and uncontrolled concealment of the mind.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:33"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hidden life proceeds toward birth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Generation moves through concealed interior, sexual readiness, conception, gestation, visible swelling, and the appointed time of emergence.","parent_surprising_reach":"The process connects womb, menstrual interval, animal heat, hoped-for child, near delivery, fruit ripening, grafting, and failed fertility.","key":"A","name":"Womb, concealed fetus, and gathered bodily matter","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An interior chamber gathers blood or seed and shelters developing life.","active_motifs":"الجنين المستور في البطن (`quranic:root_000266:B007/m01`); الجنان المستور في الصدر (`quranic:root_000266:B010/m01`); الجناجن عظام الصدر (`quranic:root_000266:B016/m01`); قرء الحيض والطهر (`quranic:root_001210:B003/m01`); رحم يجمع حملا (`quranic:root_001210:B004/m01`); وقت الشيء ودورانه (`quranic:root_001211:B002/m01`); اجتماع الرحم وحمله (`quranic:root_001211:B003/m01`); لقاح جزئي أو حمل الناقة (`quranic:root_000819:B012/m01`); دخول ثابت في الخلال (`quranic:root_001102:B001/m01`); إمساك للاستبراء أو حبس (`quranic:root_001210:B010/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ)","synthesis":"Fetus, inward heart-chamber, chest bones, menstrual or pure interval, womb-gathered pregnancy, partial conception, penetration, and detention for pregnancy verification form a concealed reproductive assembly. The body is both container and timed process.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:19","69:25","69:30"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Hidden life proceeds toward birth","parent_semantic_invariant":"Generation moves through concealed interior, sexual readiness, conception, gestation, visible swelling, and the appointed time of emergence.","parent_surprising_reach":"The process connects womb, menstrual interval, animal heat, hoped-for child, near delivery, fruit ripening, grafting, and failed fertility.","key":"C","name":"Approaching birth, first growth, and ripened issue","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A hidden process becomes externally legible as its time and product approach.","active_motifs":"قرب النتاج (`quranic:root_000493:B004/m01`); جن الشيء في بدايته (`quranic:root_000266:B014/m01`); دنو القِطاف وحلول وقته (`quranic:root_001241:B003/m01`); إدراك الثمر وأخذ الطعم (`quranic:root_000934:B005/m01`); إطعام الغصن وقبول الوصل (`quranic:root_000934:B010/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); هناءة الماشية من النبت (`quranic:root_001604:B003/m01`); تتابع الخلق (`quranic:root_000934:B014/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ); `ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `ه ن ء`: 69:24 (هَنِيٓـًٔۢا)","synthesis":"Near delivery, the first stage of youth, harvest time, fruit taking flavor, successful graft, emerging produce, an animal's share of fresh growth, and successive formation all express maturation becoming available at the boundary.","ayah_refs":["69:23","69:22","69:34","69:36","69:19","69:25","69:24"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Speech moves between utterance, belief, and verdict","parent_semantic_invariant":"A statement can be voiced, circulated, negotiated, held inwardly, treated as a proposition, or imposed as judgment.","parent_surprising_reach":"Speech becomes tongue, reputation, bargaining, private thought, logical definition, song, excessive talk, and a sharp instrument of authority.","key":"E","name":"Particle, interrogative substitute, and letter-name","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Small grammatical forms orient clauses, questions, negations, and written sound.","active_motifs":"حرف عَلَى وما جرى مجراه (`quranic:root_001042:B012/m01`); ها بدل ألف الاستفهام (`quranic:root_001573:B004/m01`); الهاء حرف هجاء (`quranic:root_001573:B006/m01`); ليس جحود ينفي الحال كفعل جامد (`quranic:root_001390:B001/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `ه ا ء`: unavailable (surface root missing); `ل ي س`: 69:35 (لَيْسَ)","synthesis":"The preposition `ala`, a deictic form replacing an interrogative alif, the name of the letter ha, and grammatical negation form a compact language-mechanics scene. The `ه ا ء` forms have no available surface-root anchor.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:35"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"A","name":"Wrapping, underlayer, and hidden load","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Cloth encloses the body and creates an inner layer in which another object may be concealed.","active_motifs":"لف الثوب واشتماله (`quranic:root_000819:B003/m01`); حمل مستور في الاشتمال (`quranic:root_000819:B005/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); السلفة بطانة الخف (`quranic:root_000733:B013/m01`); القطيفة ذات الخمل وما يشبهها (`quranic:root_001241:B007/m01`); الستر والاستتار (`quranic:root_000266:B001/m01`); الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); مواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ق ط ف`: 69:23 (قُطُوفُ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ)","synthesis":"Cloak, object hidden under the cloak, undergarment, shoe lining, plush cover, generic concealment, shield, and burial covering organize textile by layers. Protection and concealment are neighboring but distinct uses of the same enclosing surface.","ayah_refs":["69:25","69:30","69:24","69:23","69:22"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Cloth is wrapped, lined, threaded, and worn","parent_semantic_invariant":"Textile construction proceeds from joined material through inner lining, thread-path, cut edge, wrapping, and protective or deceptive surface.","parent_surprising_reach":"Garment making reaches waterskin stitching, shoe lining, plush bedding, hidden sword, under-armor layer, leather residue, and cloth whose colors misrepresent its state.","key":"D","name":"Protective covering and bodily stilling","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"A made covering shields a vulnerable body or arrests its movement.","active_motifs":"الجُنّة الواقية (`quranic:root_000266:B008/m01`); مواراة الميت (`quranic:root_000266:B009/m01`); إسكان الذبيحة بالسكين (`quranic:root_000726:B007/m01`); شد وعاء يشتمل على الحيوان أو النخلة (`quranic:root_000819:B004/m01`); طوق يغل العضو (`quranic:root_001102:B006/m01`); غلالة بين الثياب (`quranic:root_001102:B007/m01`); الأخذ بالذراع وتذريعه (`quranic:root_000512:B007/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ش م ل`: 69:25 (شِمَالِ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ذ ر ع`: 69:32 (ذَرْعُ/ذِرَاعًا)","synthesis":"Shield, burial cover, knife, tied udder cover, iron collar, under-armor layer, and arm-restraint all use material at the body boundary. Protection and punishment diverge according to whether the covering intercepts harm or fixes the person inside it.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:34","69:25","69:30","69:32"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Water is routed from channel to throat","parent_semantic_invariant":"Water moves through runoff, channel, basin, vessel, throat, body, washing, and irrigated ground.","parent_surprising_reach":"The channel includes foreign floodwater, a ruddered vessel, honeycomb, smooth drink, saline water, seasoned skin, hidden valley stream, bath, and lush pasture.","key":"A","name":"Runoff, channel, and incoming yield","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Water arrives from elsewhere, is directed through a course, and produces growth at its destination.","active_motifs":"مجرى الماء وتسليك سبيله (`quranic:root_000009:B004/m01`); السيل الآتي من غير البلد (`quranic:root_000009:B005/m01`); خروج النماء والنتاج (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); ماء وموضع ونبت في الشجر (`quranic:root_001102:B003/m01`); أرض ونبت ريان (`quranic:root_000783:B014/m01`); البستان المستور بالشجر (`quranic:root_000266:B003/m01`); التفاف النبات واندفاعه (`quranic:root_000266:B011/m01`); السلوف في أوائل الإبل (`quranic:root_000733:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ء ت ي`: 69:19 (أُوتِىَ), 69:25 (أُوتِىَ/أُوتَ); `غ ل ل`: 69:30 (غُلُّ); `ش ر ب`: 69:24 (ٱشْرَبُ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ)","synthesis":"Channel, imported runoff, increased water or yield, hidden stream among trees, moist ground, garden, dense vegetation, and lead animal at the watering place form a routed irrigation scene. Arrival becomes productive because the flow is guided rather than merely abundant.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:25","69:30","69:24","69:22"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Punishment is mapped onto limbs and inner organs","parent_semantic_invariant":"Control and suffering are localized at hand, arm, neck, throat, back, eye, belly, bone, and breath.","parent_surprising_reach":"The anatomy reaches speech organs, a padded seat, hip enlargement, eye inflammation, vomiting, colic, and the amount a forearm can physically span.","key":"F","name":"Epidemic, madness, and bitter medicine","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Disease can spread through a place, cover the mind, and call for an aversive treatment.","active_motifs":"قرأة البلاد ووبؤها (`quranic:root_001210:B008/m01`); ستر العقل بالجنون (`quranic:root_000266:B006/m01`); الحُضُض دواء مر (`quranic:root_000334:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ح ض ض`: 69:34 (يَحُضُّ)","synthesis":"Regional epidemic, mind covered by madness, and bitter medicinal resin form a compact diagnostic-treatment scene spanning place, cognition, and remedy.","ayah_refs":["69:19","69:22","69:34"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Time advances from prior act to final due point","parent_semantic_invariant":"Earlier deed, recurring interval, ripening, due time, and termination form a causal chronology.","parent_surprising_reach":"Temporal order is materialized as menstruation, animal heat, harvest, advance meal, debt maturity, fever's due moment, and waterskin decay.","key":"D","name":"Aging, first stage, and deteriorated material","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"Time appears in bodies and artifacts as youth, maturity, age, and decay.","active_motifs":"جن الشيء في بدايته (`quranic:root_000266:B014/m01`); المرأة المسلف (`quranic:root_000733:B012/m01`); الشيخ المنسوب إلى كُنْتُ (`quranic:root_001332:B005/m01`); بلى السقاء لطول الترك (`quranic:root_001237:B007/m01`); الحمى والحمام في الأبدان والأرض (`quranic:root_000001:B005/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `س ل ف`: 69:24 (أَسْلَفْ); `ك و ن`: 69:27 (كَانَتِ), 69:33 (كَانَ); `ق ض ي`: 69:27 (قَاضِيَةَ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ)","synthesis":"Beginning of youth, mature woman, elder who speaks in the past tense, decayed waterskin, and fevered body show time as alteration of capacity and material integrity.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:24","69:27","69:33","69:35"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Social rank is built from household, approval, and reputation","parent_semantic_invariant":"Standing in a community depends on household attachment, recognized character, mutual approval, public reputation, and the ability to provide.","parent_surprising_reach":"The field includes householder, poor resident, divorced woman, in-law, guarantor, singer, sharp speaker, honor inherited from ancestors, and a mountain used as a personal name.","key":"D","name":"Crowd, dependent property, and provision-bearing group","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"People, dependents, and livestock gather as a resource-bearing social body.","active_motifs":"سواد الناس وجماعتهم (`quranic:root_000266:B013/m01`); القِرَة مال وعيال (`quranic:root_001210:B013/m01`); الحميم والحامة في القربى (`quranic:root_000001:B011/m01`); كرائم المال والإبل (`quranic:root_000001:B014/m01`); أهل الدار (`quranic:root_000726:B003/m01`); قوت يثبت المقام (`quranic:root_000726:B010/m01`); رزق ومعاش وحسن حال (`quranic:root_000934:B004/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ج ن ن`: 69:22 (جَنَّةٍ); `ق ر ء`: 69:19 (ٱقْرَءُ); `ح م م`: 69:35 (حَمِيمٌ); `س ك ن`: 69:34 (مِسْكِينِ); `ط ع م`: 69:34 (طَعَامِ), 69:36 (طَعَامٌ)","synthesis":"Mass of people, property and dependents, close kin, choice stock, household, sustaining pasture, and livelihood describe a group whose bonds are simultaneously emotional and economic.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:19","69:35","69:34","69:36"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Vertical rank moves between dominance and recovery","parent_semantic_invariant":"Height can signify honor, coercive superiority, an upper tool-position, a summons upward, or recovery from a lowered bodily state.","parent_surprising_reach":"The vertical field reaches tyrannical ascent, mastery, a call to approach, anvil and spear-tip, prepositional 'upon,' low ground at a mountain foot, baseness, and rising after illness.","key":"A","name":"Arrogant ascent and coercive superiority","reading_type":"mixed","scene_or_process":"An agent turns elevation into mastery over others.","active_motifs":"العظمة والتجبر (`quranic:root_001042:B003/m01`); الغلبة والاستيلاء (`quranic:root_001042:B004/m01`); السلاطة قوة قاهرة مسلطة (`quranic:root_000732:B001/m01`); السلطان قدرة وولاية (`quranic:root_000732:B003/m01`); الدناءة والضعة (`quranic:root_000493:B003/m01`)","ayah_anchors":"`ع ل و`: 69:22 (عَالِيَةٍ); `س ل ط`: 69:29 (سُلْطَٰنِ); `د ن و`: 69:23 (دَانِيَةٌ)","synthesis":"Arrogant elevation, dominance, coercive capacity, delegated rule, and social baseness establish a hierarchy whose upper and lower positions are ethically charged.","ayah_refs":["69:22","69:29","69:23"]},{"parent_channel":"P2: Vertical rank moves between dominance and recovery","parent_semantic_invariant":"Height can signify honor, coercive superiority, an upper tool-position, a summons upward, or recovery from a lowered bodily state.","parent_surprising_reach":"The vertical field reaches tyrannical ascent, mastery, a call to approach, anvil and spear-tip, prepositional 'upon,' low ground at a mountain foot, baseness, and rising after illness.","key":"B","name":"Upward summons and elevated implement","reading_type":"latent/lexical","scene_or_process":"An upper point or tool-position organizes approach and work.","active_motifs":"نداء التعالي (`quranic:root_001042:B006/m01`); ## Your response

Write in tr. Produce your response as three parts, in this order. If you return one combined response, label the parts clearly. If an orchestrator asks you to write the parts into separate files, omit wrapper labels from the prose and evidence files; the file path already supplies the label.

1. **The prose** — per the output contract in `COMMENTARY_SPEC.md` §5 and the task document above: continuous, single voice, no provenance markers, no headers named after evidence layers.
2. **The evidence surface** — separate from the prose, addressable per phrase, per `PRINCIPLES.md` §12.

## V2 Rendering Profile — 5.6 Sol High

This run tests whether `5.6-sol-high` can increase lexical depth while preserving
reader-facing clarity.

Increase depth through semantic precision, not additional bulk.

Before drafting, silently build a coverage ledger:

- include every `must_integrate` topic;
- include every `candidate` with a reader payoff not already expressed;
- if `v12_reader_responses` is absent because the default workflow retired
  per-ayah focus runs, do not infer a `stage_00` isolated response or
  confidence movement;
- if `v12_focus_trace_hermetic` is present, use its `baseline_models`,
  `context_deltas`, and `surprising_valid_outliers` as reconstructed
  before/after evidence. Do not call it a staged reveal transcript. Preserve
  surprising outliers when they remain anchored in this ayah, especially
  secondary split-root activations;
- include a regular reader-walk, plus/minus-5 reader-walk, whole-surah reading,
  or cross-run-publication item only when it adds a distinct retrospective
  insight or coverage check;
- use branch inventories to support explanations; they create no standalone
  prose obligation. When fallback inventories are present, treat them as
  restricted to this ayah's roots and anchored citations, not as a whole-surah
  obligation.
- treat `root_lexicon` as full dictionary/gloss support, not branch-filtered.
  It uses QAC-to-Furuq root mapping; when a QAC root is split, non-dominant
  Furuq targets are included as additional root entries and recorded in
  coverage. If multiple QAC roots map to the same Furuq root, the shared entry
  records all source roots in `qac_roots_ar` / `qac_root_mappings`; use those
  fields for evidence attribution when present, not the legacy single `root_ar`
  alone. Dictionary/gloss branches are evidence support, not independent
  obligations, unless tied to this ayah's word, topic, reader payoff, or cited
  relation. Full field means all distinct reader payoffs from activated
  material, not every dictionary branch for the root.
- if `channel_generated_outputs` lists quran-data files and this run gives you
  file access, read only those listed files when channel-family/path detail is
  necessary. Treat them as candidate/family/path evidence, not as an adjudicated
  channel ledger. State B channel restrictions still apply.

For each critical word, preserve these distinct layers when available:

1. local grammatical work;
2. locally selected sense;
3. coherent pressure supplied by activated or cited root branches;
4. one form, sound, rarity, or variant observation with a unique payoff;
5. later contextual change, compressed to its final reader-visible result.

Do not flatten these layers into a general metaphor. Deepen generic summaries by
naming the exact lexical mechanism. Ordering grammar and local meaning first is
grounding, not truth-ranking.

At first mention of an ayah word, use a structured Arabic surface span:
`{ar:surface_form, tr:Turkish-readable transliteration, gloss:target-language meaning}`.
Use the same full span again when the prose returns to that word after moving to
another word or another paragraph. Inside one short local sequence, after a full
span has just been given, a Turkish label or transliteration is enough. Explain
every technical term in the same sentence. Keep raw roots, root skeletons,
letter-by-letter root transliterations, and branch IDs in the evidence surface.
In prose, attach root discussion to the surface word:
`{ar:ٱلْعَادِيَاتِ, tr:el-âdiyât, gloss:koşup atılanlar} kelimesinin bağlı
olduğu kök alanı...`, not `ʿ-d-v kökü...`.

A paragraph may begin with the ayah's surface, a concrete image, or a
reader-facing claim. It may not begin with a bare grammar label or stacked
abstractions. Prefer "Âyet önce hamdi Allah'a verir; sabit ad cümlesi bu hamdi
yerleşik bir hüküm olarak taşır" over "Bu âyet, tek bir isim cümlesiyle yerleşik
bir hüküm kurar."

For this short ayah, keep at least two-thirds of the prose on its own wording.
Compress all later developments into at most three paragraphs and end with one
plain synthesis paragraph.

Use `v12_cross_run_publication`, if present, only as a compact coverage/priority
check derived from regular and plus/minus-5 reader runs. Do not copy it as prose,
and do not let it override local bundle evidence.

Use an explicit negative predicate only to correct a likely misconception,
protect the primary sense from replacement, or preserve live counter-evidence.
Default ceiling for this ayah: three. Never stack two negatives in one sentence.
During final revision, rewrite all other negatives as positive predication.
Do not drive negation to zero by default: if no explicit negative is needed,
include a profile-specific style audit in friction confirming that there was no
live misconception, replacement risk, or counter-evidence requiring one.

Pass only if every paragraph has one distinct reader payoff and the prose remains
clear with the evidence surface closed.
